Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in boltzmann theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 612 and the invariant I(A,B) = 124 mod 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 612 + O(1/n^124). Summing over all modes and applying the boltzmann convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
